      Calculations: 
         TempCorrFDOMQSU: TempCorrFDOMQSU = FDOMQSU ./ ( 1 + -0.01 .* ( TempC - 25 ) )
         TurbCorrFDOMQSU: TurbCorrFDOMQSU = TempCorrFDOMQSU ./ ( 2.71828182845904 .^ ( -0.006 .* 
            TurbidityFNU ) )

            TurbCorrFDOMQSU

      Variable Definitions: 
          1. Fractional MATLAB serial day (based on 1 = January 1, 0000)
          2. 
          3. Measurement of RECORD
          4. Instantaneous measurement of Nitrate_mg
          5. Instantaneous measurement of Temperature in-stream with YSI EXO2 sonde
          6. Instantaneous measurement of Conductivity in-stream with YSI EXO2 sonde
          7. Instantaneous measurement of Specific Conductivity in-stream with YSI EXO2 sonde
          8. Instantaneous measurement of pH in-stream with YSI EXO2 sonde
          9. Instantaneous measurement of Optical Dissolved Oxygen (% saturation) in-stream with 
             YSI EXO2 sonde
         10. Instantaneous measurement of Optical Dissolved Oxygen (mg/L) in-stream with YSI EXO2 
            sonde
         11. Instantaneous measurement of Turbidity (FNU) in-stream with YSI EXO2 sonde
         12. Instantaneous measurement of Fluorescent Dissolved Organic Matter (RFU) in-stream with 
            YSI EXO2 sonde
         13. Instantaneous measurement of Fluorescent Dissolved Organic Matter (QSU ppb) in-stream 
            with YSI EXO2 sonde
         14. Instantaneous dissolved pCO2 measured by K30 CO2 meter in equilibrated housing with 
            air bubbler.
         15. Instantaneous atmospheric CO2 meausured by K30 CO2 meter.
         16. Instantaneous stage height measured by HOBO pressure logger, corrected for barometric 
            pressure.
         17. Instantaneous discharge calculated using stage height - discharge rating curve 
            relationship. Discharge measurements for rating curve taken using both Flow Tracker ADV 
            and IQ ADCP.
         18. Instantaneous measurement of nitrate concentration, corrected for site-specific (DCF) 
            SUNA bias. 
         19. Instantaneous Photosynthetically Active Radiation measured by Odyssey logger.
         20. Absorbance at 254nm from SUNA light frame spectral data
         21. Temperature, turbidity, and absorbance corrected fDOM
         22. Instantaneous measurement of Nitrate (mg N/L) in-stream with YSI EXO2 Sonde
